    animation-duration: 120ms;
    -webkit-animation-timing-function: ease-in-out;
    animation-timing-function: ease-in-out;
    -webkit-animation-direction: normal;
    animation-direction: normal;

    animation-delay: 0s;
}

    0% {
        opacity: 0;
        left: 100%;
        -webkit-transform: translateX(100%,0);
        -ms-transform: translateX(100%,0);
        transform: translateX(100%,0);
        -webkit-animation-timing-function: ease-in-out;
        animation-timing-function: ease-in-out;
    } 

    100% {
        opacity: 1;
        left: 0;
        -webkit-transform: translateX(0,0);
        -ms-transform: translateX(  0,0);
        transform: translateX(0,0);
        -webkit-animation-timing-function: ease-in-out;
        animation-timing-function: ease-in-out;
    }
}

    0% {
        opacity: 1;
        left: 0;
        -webkit-transform: translateX(0,0);
        -ms-transform: translateX(0,0);

<script>
    function getCountryCode(){
        return 'LU';
    }
    function getLanguageCode(){
        return 'en';
    }
    function getApiHost(){
        return 'https:\/\/api.flysas.com\/';
    }
	function getExternalizedRootPage(){
		return 'https:\/\/www.flysas.com\/en';
	}
	function getLocale(){
        return 'en';
	}
	function getPOS() {
		var pos = getCountryCode();
		if(getCountryCode() == 'BLUEPRINT'){
			pos = 'SE';
		} else if (pos.length == 0 || pos.length > 2) {
        	pos = 'LU';
        }
        return pos;
    }
</script>

  function read_cookie(key)
  {
      var result;
      return (result = new RegExp('(?:^|; )' + encodeURIComponent(key) + '=([^;]*)').exec(document.cookie)) ? (result[1]) : null;
  }
  var language = read_cookie('_language');
     if (language !== undefined) {
     document.getElementsByTagName('html')[0].setAttribute('lang', language);
     }
